Some references exist in archived .httrack.bak files or download helper scripts. These are identified as archival/helper records in JSON and should remain historical evidence rather than being edited as live UI.

Preservation and approval boundaries

Keep all CSS, fonts, responsive sizes, layout/spacing, colours, motion timing, camera paths, interactions, generic room/TV backgrounds, flora/ground textures and 3D rendering code unchanged. The inventory identifies content dependencies; it does not authorize replacing them. Cached stock imagery is fully enumerated in the asset index and mapped by local-image-cache.json. Generic scenery and client/agency content must not be mistaken for the original person’s portrait.

Future approval groups: (1) name/contact/social; (2) portraits and portrait icons; (3) character imagery; (4) personal marks and badge; (5) profile videos; (6) CV/PDF and biography; (7) individual projects/artworks; (8) metadata and routing; (9) contact delivery; (10) separate agency identity, if requested. No replacement content has been applied.

Inspection limits: text/code, file metadata, both PDF pages, identity assets, 34 artwork thumbnails, 15 agency posters and three sample frames of each of 14 videos were reviewed. Not every cached stock image or every video frame was OCR-reviewed. Dynamic labels and embedded raster text require a final section-specific review before future changes.
